Well : AZNS??? (S358)

Type : ISHPS

SEQUENCE OF OPERATION Page 31 of 117

Development Well

26” Hole Section

D.1.1. General

Casing Point 20” Shoe @ 154m

Formation Aghajari

Risks &amp;amp; Hole problems Bit Balled

Deviation Control Inclinometer

Logging No Logging Program

Drilling Fluid

Pre-hydrate Bentonite

MW 67 pcf

Vis. 70-80 sec

PV 10-12

YP 35-40

Gel 15/20

PH 11

Drilling Partameters

GPM 300-900

WOB 10-30 KLBs

RPM 80-100

D.1.2. Sequence of Operation

 Make up 26” BHA as per BHA design. Tag formation at ±14m.

 Drill ahead formation down to 154Mt (20” casing point in Aghajari formation) using hivis bentonite pill. Pay enough attention to drill straight hole.

Note: TD should be adjusted to have 1m of rat hole below casing shoe.

- The exact TD will be determined based on casing tally.

- Run Inclinometer each 50m to evaluate the verticality of the hole.

- Ensure that all drilling tools and equipment are on well site and are in serviceable

condition.

- Ensure that adequate fishing tools for this section of hole are available.

- Enough Mud Material, LCM and Cement Should be Available before spud the rig to use

in case of needing.

- 1 Set cement pump truck should be available on well site before start drilling.

- Mud Logging unit should be available from rig spud.

- At TD, Circulate and clean the hole. Take Survey, POOH to surface. Check Bit.

 Perform a reamer trip due to conditioning the hole prior to run casing. If observe any

indication of drag, run back to bottom. Wash and ream to make sure that the hole

condition is good.

Well : AZNS??? (S358)

Type : ISHPS

SEQUENCE OF OPERATION Page 32 of 117

Development Well

 Perform Safety meeting.

 R/U casing running equipment.

 Run ±154m (Approx. 15 Jts) 20", 106.5 ppf, K-55, BTC casing with duplex shoe to

bottom as per casing program. Casing shoe should be adjusted to have 1m of rat hole and

enough stick up (1 m on rig floor). Fill casing by non LCM fresh mud.

Note: Take care when running casing not to set down excessive weight if tight hole or

ledges are encountered.

 Run stinger on DP’s using the “C” Plate &amp;amp; 5” x 20” centralizer. Sting in. M/U &amp;amp; test

cementing lines. Circulate for one casing annulus volume. While circulating, check the

DPs annulus for any return. Use all DPs weight on the string if observing any minor

leakage through DPs annulus.

 Cement casing as per program. Any minor adjustment regarding the corrected depth will

be done in due time. Displace with mud (1-2 bbl. Short Displace). Monitor for pressures.

Record and report the final pressure, Sting out. Check the float. POOH one stand,

Circulate to flush/clean drill pipe.

 After cement returns to cellar floor, open the 2" nipple on conductor &amp;amp; flush it.

 POOH to surface. L/D stinger.

 Keep the casing on the elevator until cement surface samples get hard. Check for casing

to be center inside conductor pipe.

 Note: Based on the results obtained from cementing, decision will be made to

perform top-up jobs.

- If there is no cement return to surface, a top-up job should be performed to fill the annulus.

Use same slurry weight as the primary cement slurry. Ensure the availability of adequate

X-over for connecting line pipe to cement line. The cement should be accelerated with

max. (2%) Calcium Chloride BWOC and no other additives. Wait for the top job cement

to get hard.

 Check Cement by slack off weight.

 Cut conductor pipe. Rough cut landing joint and L/D C.P. and L.J. Dress and bevel 20”

suitable to install and to weld casing head spool.

 Install and weld casing head housing 20-3/4” – 3000 psi on casing and pressure test the

sealing of welded casing. (Limit pressure to a maximum of 80% of the collapse

strength of the casing). Outlets must be aligning with cellar stairs.

Note:

- Check the 20 3/4” wear bushing, 20 3/4” Plug Tester and 13-3/8” Casing Slip and seal

assembly in CHH before installing CHH (on Surface).

 If cellar is standard the distance between its top flange and ground level must be 1.73m.

 If cellar is not standard the base of CHH should not be more than 10cm upper than the

floor of cellar.

 N/U Double Studded Tie Down Flange 20-3/4” 3K x 21 1/4" 2K + 21-1/4” 2K Spacer

Spool + 21 1/4" 2K Double Rams and 21-1/4” Annular Preventer 2K and related lines

and valves and test all to 2500psi.

Well : AZNS??? (S358)

Type : ISHPS

SEQUENCE OF OPERATION Page 33 of 117

Development Well

 N/U Bell Nipple &amp;amp; flow line

Note: The height of spacer spool must be designed untill the outlet of spool placed above

the ground level.

 The rig should be centered again, before starting the next hole operation.

 P/U &amp;amp; M/U Next BHA and stand drill pipes to 1370m.

- Fill up the following forms and send to drilling office:

- 20” Casing Running Report

- 20” CSG Cement Report

D.2.2. Sequence of Operation

 Make up 17-1/2” BHA as per BHA Design. RIH, tag top of cement. Drill out cement &amp;amp;

shoe and rat hole cement.

 Watch carefully for any indication of abnormal torque, since there might be a possibility

of shoe being backed off. Report problem immediately.

 Drill ahead formation down to 178m (24m below 20” casing shoe) with fresh 67 pcf Mud

as program.

 Circulate BTM’s up to clean the hole. Displace the hole w/ 70 pcf kcl/polymer mud.

 Drill ahead 17-1/2” hole section down to casing point @ ±1275m, at the top of Gachsaran

to case off full soft unconsolidated formation of lower Aghajari and drill the next hole

section with Higher mud density and flow zones.

Note:

- Adjust mud weight according to program and as hole condition dictate.

- Single shot every 100 m and multi-shot every 300m.

- The mud engineer shall check the mud properties as pre-planned time schedule

especially for (Ca++, THD and Chloride, pH, F.L, Gels, Mud weight and FV).

Well : AZNS??? (S358)

Type : ISHPS

SEQUENCE OF OPERATION Page 35 of 117

Development Well

- TD should be adjusted so that to have 2 meters of rat hole for probable fillings, before

running casing.

- Section TD maybe changed based on formation tops detected by well site geologist.

- Choke manifold and choke line must be lined up for SOFT SHUT-IN through pit or

burning line.

- Ensure that adequate fishing tools for this section are available.

- Ensure that enough amount and type of LCM are available and keep ready to mix and

pump LCM Pills to cure losses.

- At section TD, circulate, clean hole. Perform wiper trip to shoe and observe well @

shoe. RIH, Check for any fill. CBU on bottom. Spot 100 bbls hi-vis pill @ bottom.

 POOH to surface, if observed any indication of tight hole, run back to TD. W&amp;amp;R to

make sure that the hole condition is good prior to run casing.

 Perform pre-job safety meeting.

 Run CDR/GR Log in open hole.

Note: If hole condition was not okay, logging operation can be performed after running

casing.

 R/U casing running equipment.

 Run casing as per casing program. Run ±1275 (Approx. 135 Jts) 13 3/8", 68 ppf, N-80,

BTC casing.

 Casing shoe depth should be adjusted so that to have 1 meters of rat hole and enough

stick up (1.5 m) on rig floor.

Note:

- Ensure that the casing is drifted; cleaned and tallied priors to RIH.

- Fill casing by non LCM fresh mud.

- Take care when running casing not to set down excessive weight if tight hole or ledges

are encountered.

 When casing is at casing setting depth, circulate while preparing for cementing. M/U &amp;amp;

test cementing lines. Circulate for 120% of casing annulus volume.

 Cement casing as per program. Any minor adjustment regarding the corrected depth will

be done in due time. Displace with mud. Monitor for pressures. Bump plug with 1500

psi for 15min. (Max 80% Casing Burst). Record and report the final pressure.

Note:

- Based on the results obtained from cementing, decision will be made to perform top-up

jobs.

 Hold on casing string weight. Drop casing hanger. Slack off 80% of casing string

weight. Check for proper installation. Pressure test casing hanger.

 Remove the flow line and diverter. Rough cut L.J. Dress and bevel casing neck.

 Install CSG head spool 20 3/4”-3000*13 ⅝”-5000. Pack &amp;amp; Test p-seals and cavity Test

Note:

- Check the 13-5/8” wear bushing, 13-5/8” Plug Tester and 9-5/8” Casing Slip in CHS

 N/U 13 5/8” x 5000 psi BOP stack. N/U bell nipple. Test BOP stack and related lines to

D.3.1. General

Casing Point 9.625” Shoe @ 1577m

Formation Gachsaran and Cap Rock

Risks &amp;amp; Hole Problems Wash &amp;amp; Ream, Salt water Flow &amp;amp; Increase Ca++, Tight Hole

&amp;amp; Stuck

Deviation Control Inclinometer

Logging GR/CDR/DT

Salt saturated Kcl Polymer mud

MW 95-107 pcf

Vis. 60 - 75 sec.

PV ALAP

YP 15-26 Lbs/100ft²

FL &amp;lt;3

Drilling Parameters

GPM 550-800

WOB 20-40 KLBs

D.3.2. Sequence of Operation

 Make up 12-1/4” BHA as per BHA design include Junk Sub. RIH, Tag top of cement.

Drill out plugs, Float collar, shoe track, Displace hole with 95 pcf mud Drill out rat hole.

Clean out the rat hole, circulate clean. Work on junk sub.

 Perform F.I.T in Gachsaran Formation.

 Continue drilling Fm. to 5 m below shoe.

 Circulate and clean hole, Work on junk sub, POOH to surface, L/D junk sub and P/U new

bit &amp;amp; string Stabilizer @ 20m, RIH to bottom.

 Continue drilling Fm. to Casing Point @ ±1577 Mt at the top of Gachsaran to top of

Asmari in cap rock.

Note:

- Perform BOP's test every 2 weeks.

- Single shot every 100 m and multi-shot every 300m.

Well : AZNS??? (S358)

Type : ISHPS

SEQUENCE OF OPERATION Page 38 of 117

Development Well

- Choke line and choke manifold must be lined up for Soft SHUT-IN.

- Adjust mud weight according to nearby wells and as hole condition dictation.

- The Section TD maybe changed based on formation tops detected by the well site

geologist.

- Ensure that all drilling tools and equipment are on well site in serviceable condition.

- Ensure that adequate fishing tools are available for this section.

- Ensure that enough amounts of LCM are available at location.

 At TD, circulate and clean the hole prior to wiper trip. Observe the hole for one hour @

casing shoe. RIH to bottom and Pump 50 bbl hi-vis pill, circulate to clean hole (check

bottoms up for any influx), Spot 70 bbl hi-vis Pill prior to POOH.

 POOH to surface. If observed any indication of drag, run back to bottom. Wash and ream

to make sure that the hole condition is good.

 R/U wire line logging equipment. Run Wire line Logging as program CDR- GR.

 R/D wire line.

 RIH and condition hole prior to run casing and spot hi-vis pill.

 Change top rams to 9-5/8”.

 R/U casing running equipment. Perform pre-job safety meeting.

 Run casing as per casing program

 Casing shoe depth should be adjusted for having short rat hole and enough stick up (1.5

m) on rig floor. Fill casing by non LCM fresh mud each 10 joints.

 M/U cementing head. Load the plug. WSS shall witness the loading. Test the lines to

3000 psi for 10 minutes. Make sure that the cement head is capable of holding pressure

while displacing and testing cement (Visually).

 Circ 1.2 times of casing volume with low GPM up to clean the shaker.

 Perform cementing as per program. Adjustment of cement volume should be done as per

change of drilling depth at operation time. Displace with mud. Bump plug with 3000psi

for 15min. (Max 80% Casing Burst). Record and report the final pressure. Bleed off

pressure. Check for back flow. If any back flow observed, hold the pressure inside casing

until the cement is hard.

Hold on casing string weight. Drop casing hanger. Slack off 80% of casing string

weight. Close Rams. Check for proper installation. Pressure test casing slips.

 Raise BOP. Rough cut landing joint. L/D same. Dress and bevel casing neck

 T.H.S. 13 5/8”-5000 PSI x 11"–5000 PSI.

 Pack &amp;amp; Test p-seals and cavity test (Max 80% collapse)

Note:

 N/U bell nipple. Test BOP stack and related lines to 4500 psi pressure, using plug tester.

 L/D 8-1/2” drill collars, P/U &amp;amp; M/U Next BHA and stand drill pipes.

 Fill up the following forms and send them to drilling office :

D.4.2. Sequence of Operation

 Make up 8-1/2” B.Bit (217) w/ New BHA, RIH to top of 9-5/8” float collar (Top Plug).

 Drill out plugs,. float collar and cement to 2m above shoe, Displace Mud in hole with

74 pcf KCL Mud and Obs. The Well Several Times. Drill out remained cement and 9-

5/8”. Float shoe and cement of Rat hole, observe well for any Loss or flow. Watch

carefully for any indication of abnormal torque, since there might be the risk of shoe

back off. In case, report immediately.

 if the well is static drill ahead to 5m below shoe , work on junk sub, observe well.

C.B.U,

 Take CBL/VDL/CCL/Gr from 9 5/8” casing.

 Perform F.I.T

 POOH to surface, L/D J.Sub.

 P/UP New 8-1/2" PDC Bit and Drilling to 3058 (Pilot Hole TD)

 Multi shot survey shall be run in this hole section before KOP.

Development Well

 Single shot every 100 m and multi-shot every 300m.

 C.B.U and Clean the Hole , POH to Surface .

 Conditioning the hole before run log.

 Perform logging as program.

 If needed perform condition trip between logging runs.

 L/D Wire line equipment.

 RIH with Open End Drill pipe or Center Cut to TD.

 Plug back to ±30 m above KOP with 3 cement balance plugs in pilot hole. POOH.

 Plug back procedure will be issued at due time.

Note: H2S services should be available 1 weeks before reaching ILAM entry point.

Note: Enough Mud Material &amp;amp; LCM Should Be Available In Well site.

Due to High H2S Amount in This Field (Bangestan Group and Ilam Formation) All H2S

Safety Equipments and Safety Man Should be Available in location.

Note:

- Adjust mud weight according to hole condition, use nearby wells as guide line.

- During trips, while at surface, driller is to check BHA.

- A material balance to determine the theoretical concentrations of additives in the mud

should be calculated and reported to WSS. Significant deviations from programmed

values should be explained.

- Test BOP’s and related equipment as per NIOC policy each 7 Days.

 At section TD, CBU, sweep the hole cleaned, perform wiper trip.

 Spot 60 bbls of polymer high vis. pill to stabilize the hole prior to run log. Use 5-10 lbs

LCM( shell fish ), if necessary. POOH.

Full Suite Log

F.1.1. INTERVAL DISCUSION

The 26-inch section will be drilled with Lime treated drill water and hi-viscous mud

sweeps in a close system.

This is a large diameter hole and the hole cuttings removal mechanism will be a

combination of high annular velocities and hi-viscous sweeps.

Bentonite Mud/Hi-Vis Pill Gel has been planned to overcome the drilling problems and

increase the rate of penetration. Rheological properties of the system will be increased

and kept in the optimum range by adding GEL API at the concentration of 20 – 30

lb/bbl.

Active system Viscosity should be maintained by effect of PHG sweeps between 55-70

sec/qt!!! Due to low GPM at the start of drilling the section hole cleaning is important.

Soft dispersive clays and unconsolidated sands can be expected whilst drilling this

interval. The formation is mainly composed of Marl, Claystone and unconsolidated

sands. The hole is displaced with viscous mud at the section TD in order to plaster off

the sands, enhancing bore hole stability and minimizing fill while running the 20’’

casing.

This hole is to be cleaned as required but as a minimum, the following should be

adhered to:

 Every single a 15 bbls hi-viscous pill should be pumped to clean the hole.

26’’ Section

Well : AZNS??? (S358)

Type : ISHPS

MUD AND CEMENT PROGRAM Page 50 of 117

Development Well

 At connections, the 15 bbls hi-viscous pill should be spotted on bottom in order to

build filter-cake on the bore-hole wall, enhance the hole stability and prevent fill up

around bit and BHA.

Availability of drill water might be a logistical issue and shortage of proper drill

water is to be expected, therefore, Guar gum should be available to be used for mixing

hi-viscous sweeps. A large volume of fluid will have to be turned over in both this

section and next section and emphasis will be placed on pit management and drill water

logistics. The engineer should endeavor to have all the pits full with gel based hiviscous mud in order to avoid ending up in a “catching up” situation.

At section TD the following procedure shall be adhered to:

 Before pulling out of the hole to run casing the hole shall be swept with a 50 bbls PHG

viscous pill and spotted 30 m bottom of open Hole with same viscous pill.

To control mud weight, beside utilizing solid control equipment, a dump and dilute

strategy (with Lime treated drill water) should be considered.

F.1.2. POTENTIAL MUD RELATED PROBLEMS AND HAZARDS

Hole cleaning:

 Pump hi-viscous mud more frequently and of greater volume.

 Increase pump rate.

No good drill water:

 Ensure gel is pre-hydrated at high concentrations with proper drill water. Use guar gum to alternate

the gel based viscous sweeps.

Hole instability:

 Weight up drilling fluid with Barite.

F.1.3. Mud composition

Main Fluid Composition: Lime treated drill water

Drill water : base fluid

Lime : dispersion inhibitor

Mixing Procedure:

1. Fill the tanks with the required volume of drill water.

2. Add 0.5-1 ppb Lime.

PHG sweep Composition:

Drill water : Less than 200 mg/l Cl- for gel hydration.

Caustic soda : Alkalinity control

Soda Ash : Hardness control / Alkalinity control.

Well : AZNS??? (S358)

Type : ISHPS

MUD AND CEMENT PROGRAM Page 51 of 117

Development Well

Bentonite : Viscosifier

Mixing Procedure:

Note: Waste water after Dewatering should meet the minimum required properties for

making mud by considering usage of &amp;gt;50% volume in all sections.

1. Fill the tanks with the required volume of drill water.

2. Raise the pH to 9 -10 with additions of caustic soda.

3. Treat out any hardness greater than 200 mg/lit with soda ash.

4. Add 20 – 30 ppb of Bentonite and allow hydrating for as long as possible (at least 6 hours).

Processing with a shearing device will improve yield.

5. Add required Barite to adjust mud weight.

Recommended Properties:

Funnel Viscosity : ≈90 sec/qt.

Solid Control equipment use for 26” hole section:

- Shale Shakers

- Centrifuges

- Shaker Screen sizes: Mesh-80/84, Mesh-100/110, Mesh 120/140

F.2.1 INTERVAL DISCUSSION

The 17 ½’’ intervals will be drilled with Lime treated salt polymer mud and hi-viscous

mud sweeps (if necessary).

This section will be drilled through the Aghajari, Mishan and upper section of Gachsaran

formations. Lithology of mentioned formation is mainly Marl, Claystone and sand beds.

Cement in casing will be drilled with old mud and later all mud, left in previous section

(26" hole) can be used in this section too for the first few meters. Salt is used as weighting

agent and gives some inhibition against Clay swelling. Addition of Lime provides proper

pH and Calcium ion as well. Calcium ions will minimize swelling and dispersion of

formation as well.

Addition of polymers increase viscosity of fluid, decrease fluid loss and prevents

dispersion through encapsulation mechanism.

KCl might be used if hole condition dictates for more inhibition against swelling 3 % by

weight. it’s recommended using of 0.5 ppb PHPA for removing free caly and marl cuttings

from the mud system, also more concentration of PAC UL enhances mud properties

against accumulation of marl and clay around bit and stabilizers. (Compatibility and

efficiency of PHPA with high content Ca++ fluid should be checked before using)

Hole would be swept by hi-viscous PHG if any clue of cutting accumulation in annulus

(e.g drag, torque or pressure increase) is observed. The recommended volume is 20-30 bbls

17 ½’’ Section

Well : AZNS??? (S358)

Type : ISHPS

MUD AND CEMENT PROGRAM Page 53 of 117

Development Well

each time. If situation dictates pills should be dumped on bottom’s up to prevent excessive

viscosity and solid content of mud.

There is a potentially loss zone and it is recommended to have LCM pill available before

drilling through that zone. The concentration of LCM will vary according to losses

volume. Sufficient stocks of graded LCM should be kept at location. In case of seepage

losses, the system should be treated with fine grade of LCM at 25 - 30 ppb, for moderate to

severe losses, prepare pills at different graded concentrations and spot across the thief

zone.

In order to avoid LCM plugging MWD tools a circulation sub should be installed in the

drill string. The MWD engineer is to advise on the size and concentration of LCM that can

be pumped through MWD Tools. Ensure that the drilling crew displaces the entire LCM

pill out of the string prior to shutting down the mud pumps.

F.2.2. POTENTIAL PROBLEMS AND HAZARDS

Hole cleaning: to improve it, consider these

 Pump hi-viscous mud more frequently and in greater volume.

 Increase pump rate.

Hole instability, swelling and tight hole: to prevent and rectify it, note these

 Increase mud weight.

 Introduce KCl to system and increase its concentration according

to hole condition.

 Ream and back ream slowly and in case of tight hole do not push

or pull string strongly. Make sure pumps are on and work on

string slowly in case of tight point.

Down hole losses: to prevent and treat it, consider these

 Avoid cuttings accumulation in annulus, unnecessary higher mud

weight and flow rate since they insert higher ECD on formation

and may cause induced losses.

 Prepare LCM pill in advance.

Mud composition

Drill water : Base fluid

Lime : Alkalinity control

Xanthan gum (XC polymer) : Viscosifier / Rheology improver

Starch : Fluid loss control

PAC R : Viscosity / Fluid loss control

Salt : weighting agent

Mixing Procedure:

Note: Waste water after Dewatering should meet the minimum required properties

for making mud by considering usage of &amp;gt;50% volume in all sections.

Mix as per below order

1. Fill the tanks with the required volume of drill water.

2. Add 0.5 - 1 ppb Xanthan gum

3. Add 1-1.5 ppb PAC UL (Optional)

4. Add 6-8 ppb Starch (Depends on drilling depth and client instruction for FL)

5. Add 0.5 – 1 ppb Lime.

6. Add KCl at 3.5-11 ppb.

7. Add Salt to adjust mud weight till the total mud salinity doesn’t exceed from 40 gr/lit.

8. Add Barite to adjust mud weight to desired weight.

9. Add required PHPA while drilling. (Contingency)

Recommended Properties:

MW : within range around 70-80 pcf

YP : 15 – 25 lbs/100 ft2

PV : ALAP

Water loss : ≤ 8 cc/ 30 min (depends on depth will be changed)

pH : 8.5 – 9.5

MBT : &amp;lt; 20 ppb

Excess Lime : 0.5 -1 ppb

Solid Control equipment use for 17-1/2” hole section;

- Shale Shakers

- Mud Cleaner

- Centrifuges

- Shaker Screen sizes: Mesh-80/84, Mesh-100/110, Mesh 120/140/170

Engineering Comments

Pills: should be a minimum of 20-30 bbls each time. The frequency or volume may be

increased dependent upon hole conditions.

Connections: make sure before connection, there is enough circulation to remove cuttings from

around BHA.

Dilution: system should be treated periodically with light, fresh dilution to keep mud properties

in range

Bit &amp;amp; BHA Balling: Use Surfactant pill, such as Drilling detergent to reduce surface tension,

hence minimize balling. Concentration of pill is between 1 – 3 volume percent. KCl , PHPA and

more concentration of PAC UL enhance mud properties against bit and BHA balling.

Mud from this section will be transferred to the next section. Therefore, MBT should be

controlled!

F.3.1. OBJECTIVE

This section will be drilled through Gachsaran formation and few meters into Asmari

formation in order to case high pressure zone of Gachsaran.

F.3.2. INTERVAL DISCUSSIONS

Mud from previous section will be forwarded to this section and will be treated for desired

properties to drill this section.

Gachsaran consists of Marl, Clay, Anhydrite and Salt, therefore mud will be salt saturated

to minimize hole wash out and to reduce solid particles since this section will be drilled

with high mud weight.

Salt water influx with high Calcium ion concentration is expected in Gachsaran formation

and presence of Calcium in mud due to addition of Lime from beginning will prevent

drastic changes in mud properties in confrontation with high Calcium influx.

Salt saturation and addition of KCl (if needed) will minimize Clay and Marl swelling and

prevent tight point occurrence. If concentration of Calcium ions in mud changes while

drilling Anhydrite, system should be treated with Soda ash to adjust calcium concentration

and keep it at desired range.

Minimum mud weight expected for this section is 107 pcf and it will be achieved by

addition of Barite to Salt saturated mud.

Due to drilling Anhydrite and high Calcium influx, flocculation of clay/ marl should be

considered and enough stock of deflocculant chemicals e.g. Spersene should be kept at rig

site to treat system and rectify high viscosity.

12 ¼’’ Section

Well : AZNS??? (S358)

Type : ISHPS

MUD AND CEMENT PROGRAM Page 57 of 117

Development Well

Since mud weight is high and consequently viscosity is high, if it is necessary to pump

cleaning pill, it should be low-vis low-weight pill to make turbulent flow and aid hole

cleaning.

Well bore and formation related problems are identified as follows:

Hole instability: has been observed in offset wells. Make sure mud properties are in range

and mud weight is adequate to keep well bore stability and prevent tight hole.

Bit balling: This can occur due to Clay and Marl beds and is attributed to different factors

other than mud chemistry, such as bit hydraulics. At times, hydraulics is not adequate to

clean the bit and stabilizer, balling may occur. If hydraulics appears to be adequate, then a

strategy of additions of materials such as drilling detergent or walnut can be adapted in

order to minimize bit balling.

Lost circulation: The concentration of LCM will vary according to losses. Sufficient stocks

of graded LCM should be kept at location. In case of seepage losses, the system should be

treated with fine grade of LCM at 25 - 30 ppb, for moderate to severe losses, prepare pills

at different graded concentrations and spot across the thief zone.

In order to avoid LCM plugging MWD tools, a circulation sub should be installed in the

drill string. The MWD engineer is to advise on the size and concentration of LCM that can

be pumped through MWD Tools. Ensure that the drilling crew displaces all the LCM pill

out of the string prior to shutting down the mud pumps.

In the event of severe losses, crosslinking pills should be pumped to minimize losses.

Salt water influx: make sure mud weight is adequate to prevent well flowing. In case of

influx treat mud system with Soda ash to keep Calcium Ion in proper range, add fluid loss

control agents to keep fluid loss in range.

MUD COMPOSITION

Salt saturated / KCl polymer mud treated with Lime

Drill : Base fluid

KCl : Swelling Inhibitor. (if needed)

Lime : pH control.

Soda Ash : Hardness control.

Xanthan Gum (XC polymer) : Viscosifier / Rheology improver

Starch HT : Fluid loss control

Pac UL : Fluid loss control.

Pac R : Viscosifier / Fluid loss control.

Barite : Weighting agent.

Salt : Weighting agent. (Inhibitor)

Hematite : Weighting agent.(if needed)

Add 0.25-0.5 ppb Xanthan gum

Add 7 ppb Starch HT

Add 3 ppb Pac UL

Add salt to get mud weight 75 pcf (salt saturated level)

Add 0.5 – 1.5 ppb Lime, adjusted to characteristic of drilled formation.

Add Barite to get MW in range.

Note: Due to large amount of Barite consumption in 12 ¼” hole section, the centrifuges

use to be handled in Barite recovery mode for better performance, however, this system

has not been installed yet but, the possibility will be evaluated. Non-availability of Barite

recovery mode could be mostly rectified by good arrangement of shale shakers screen

dressing to minimize drill solids build up.

ENGINEERING COMMENTS

KCl Content: If cuttings integrity and well condition dictate, increase KCl content up to 3

% by weight.

Losses: surface and sub surface losses to be monitored, prepare LCM pill in advance

Well : AZNS??? (S358)

Type : ISHPS

MUD AND CEMENT PROGRAM Page 59 of 117

Development Well

Density: Drilling fluid density will be controlled by addition of Hematite / Barite or light

premix fluid. Use available solids control equipment to reduce low gravity solids build-up,

thus reducing any dilution requirements for density control. Mud weight to be increased as

hole conditions dictate. Potential problems may encounter if mud weight is not sufficient in

this section, such as;

Salt water flow

Hole instability and tight hole due to soft, plastic lithology

Plastic Viscosity: in a mud with such high weight is expected to be high, but try to keep it

ALAP.

Yield Point: in a mud with such high weight is expected to be high, but try to keep it in the

range.

Fluid loss control, API : The API fluid loss should be kept within specifications using

additions of PAC UL and /or Starch HT.

CEC: To be controlled with solids control equipment and dilution.

Hole cleaning: Although utilizing such a high weight mud should improve hole cleaning

but if any clue of cutting build up in annulus (e.g pressure increase, torque and drag) is

observed, it is recommended to pump low-vis low-weight pill to make turbulent flow and

help cutting removing.

Solids control: Ensure shakers are running immediately prior to and during all periods of

circulation. Shakers to be dressed initially with 80/100 mesh screens and after the mud is

sheared, finer screens of 120/165 mesh should be dressed. Use centrifuges as required to

control MW and LGS.

Dump and dilute strategy: Dilute active system with premixed fluid to maintain volume

and mud properties. The concentration of this fluid should be somewhat higher than in the

initial mix to compensate depletion.

Bit &amp;amp; BHA Balling: Use Surfactant pill, such as Drilling detergent to reduce surface

tension, hence minimize balling. Concentration of pill is between 1 – 3 volume percent.

Bacterial decay: before any pull out of hole, treat mud system with biocide to prevent

bacterial decay.

Foaming: drilling Anhydrite may cause foam due to flocculation of polymer and air

trapping. Treat system with Soda ash and defoamer to treat it.

Circulation and agitation: make sure pit agitators are run all the time to prevent Hematite

and Barite sag. Circulate pits with shear guns periodically to ensure sag prevention and sag

remove.

F.4.1. INTERVAL DISCUSSIONS

For drilling 8 ½" interval, a Salt /Polymer WBM system will be used and a new mud

system will be built from scratch. New mud should be mixed with CaCO3 as weighting

agent.

F.4.2. POTENTIAL MUD RELATED PROBLEMS AND HAZARDS

Well bore and formation related problems are identified as follows:

Potential losses

Differential sticking

Shale instability

Stuck Pipe: Differential pressure stuck pipe may occur due to the different gradient

between formation and mud. If stuck pipe occur, Pipe Lax spot should be pumped

according with standard Company spotting procedures.

Lost circulation: The concentration of LCM will vary according to losses. Partial to severe

losses are and sufficient stocks of LCM should be kept on board. In case of seepage

losses, the system should be treated with fine grade of LCM at 10 ppb, for moderate to

severe losses, prepare pills at different graded concentrations and spot across the thief

zone.

Well : AZNS??? (S358)

Type : ISHPS

MUD AND CEMENT PROGRAM Page 62 of 117

Development Well

In order to avoid LCM plugging MWD tools a circulation sub should be installed in the

drill string. The MWD engineer is to advise on the size and concentration of LCM that can

be pumped through MWD Tools. Ensure that the drilling displace the entire LCM pill out

of the string prior to shutting down the mud pumps.

Shale Instability: KCl and Salt will inhibit the shale swelling and PACs will encapsulate

the Shale cuttings in this section. Also high concentration of Chlorite, generated from NaCl

addition will cause to minimize Water Activity with accordance to the formation water

activity in order to create more inhibition while drilling Shale layers out.

F.4.3. MUD COMPOSITION

Well bore and formation related problems are identified as follows:

Potential losses

Differential sticking

Shale instability

Stuck Pipe: Differential pressure stuck pipe may occur due to the different gradient

between formation and mud. If stuck pipe occur, Pipe Lax spot should be pumped

according with standard Company spotting procedures.

Lost circulation: The concentration of LCM will vary according to losses. Partial to severe

losses are and sufficient stocks of LCM should be kept on board. In case of seepage

losses, the system should be treated with fine grade of LCM at 10 ppb, for moderate to

severe losses, prepare pills at different graded concentrations and spot across the thief

zone.

In order to avoid LCM plugging MWD tools a circulation sub should be installed in the

drill string. The MWD engineer is to advise on the size and concentration of LCM that can

be pumped through MWD Tools. Ensure that the drilling displace the entire LCM pill out

of the string prior to shutting down the mud pumps.

Shale Instability: KCl, Glycol (as contingency) and Salt will inhibit the shale swelling and

PACs will encapsulate the Shale cuttings in this section. Also high concentration of

Chlorite, generated from NaCl addition will cause to minimize Water Activity with

accordance to the formation water activity in order to create more inhibition while drilling

Shale layers out.

Preparation before Cementing Job

1) Equipment inspection and maintenance.

2) Pump rate and other instrument calibration.

CONTRACTOR shall:

1. Perform while the DRILLING/COMPLETION OPERATION is ongoing, all related services such as

design, supply, execute, supervise and control the waste water, mud and drill cuttings treatment plan

compatible with Iranian Petroleum Standards (IPS) as well as good engineering practices.

2. Submit its drilling waste management program,

3. Provide solidification treatment units and/or other proposed methods/units to dispose the drilling

waste materials (liquid and solid including used drilling and completion fluids, drilling cuttings

materials, residual acid and sometimes small amounts of oil).

4. Provide all related equipment, personnel and necessary chemicals for the waste treatment

program.

5. Maintain and/or repair all the related equipment.

6. Provide transportation vehicles for the treated wastes from the LOCATION to designated area

specified by COMPANY.

7. Ensure the discharge water meets following standard after dewatering:

- pH=6~9

- COD&amp;lt;200 mg/L, BOD&amp;lt;100 mg/L

- Cl-&amp;lt;600 mg/L

- Color (Color unit, Chromaticity) &amp;lt;75

- IPS-E-SF-880, National Effluent Standard for Industry Waste (AGRICULTURE AND IRRIGATION)

8. Dried and fixed cutting shall meet the standards of IPS-G-SF-130.

Drilling waste management plan shall be developed and implemented, and appropriate actions shall

be taken to prevent environmental pollution, conserve resources and minimize waste. CONTRACTOR

shall continually strive to minimize the impact of their WORK on the environment. Plans shall be

enveloped where elimination of a pollution source is not practical, appropriate treatment of waste shall

Well: AZNS??? (S358)

Type : ISHPS

WASTE MANAGEMENT PROGRAM Page 83 of 117

Development Well

be undertaken and monitored to minimize the impact of discharges and disposals on the environment.

The waste treatment and disposal shall be performed during the Drilling period. The system should

consist of following separation, containment and handling equipment, and treatment and disposal.

1. Separation equipment: high efficiency solid and liquid separation system as described in

Schedule IV.

2. Containment and handling equipment:

 Conveyer: for collection and discharge of all cuttings.

 drying shale shaker: giving dryer cuttings and reduced mud consumption.

 Contingency tanks: Arrangement for the occasional collection, separation and isolation of cutting

wastes.

 Fluid sump: for the collection of site drains and excess drilling fluid.

 Tanks: for the storage of excess fluid prior of treatment.

 Pumps: for transfer fluids.

 Backhoe, dump truck, etc. for the transportation of disposals

3. Treatment and disposal:

 Fixation: using cement or similar to stabilize the salt or chemicals.

 dewatering of excess fluids, site water, and water based drilling fluid.

Disposal area will be designated by COMPANY and CONTRACTOR shall transfer fixed cuttings to this

location at CONTRACTOR‟s cost.

CONTRACTOR shall refer to the following standards for the effluent limits.

 IPS-E-PR-735 Engineering Standard for Process Design of Plant Solid Waste

Treatment and Disposal Systems.

 IPS-E-PR-730 Engineering Standard for Process Design Plant Waste-Water

 IPS-G-SF-130 General Standard for Disposal of Solid Waste.

 IPS-E-SF-880 General Standard for Water Pollution Control.

Waste Treatment Plan

Drilling waste management plan is the ideal and best practice for minimizing, recycling and

disposal of drilling waste. Drilling Waste Management is planned based on the major produced

wastes during the drilling operation. These wastes are

 Waste Water / Contaminated Water in the pit

 Drilling fluids

 Drilling Cuttings

The minimum requirement for implementing this plan is as below;

1- Improving Solids Control Efficiency and Monitoring

2- No waste pit at rig site ( All the waste should be transferred to the special waste tanks)

3- Dewatering Unit including Flocculation Unit &amp;amp; High Speed Centrifuge

4- Clarification unit

5- Bio-Treatment practices for discharge water

6- Filtration Unit to purified recovered water phase

7- Conveyer System for Cuttings Transfer

8- Cuttings Dryer System

10- Cuttings Box Collection

11- Reserve Tanks for recycled water

12- Waste water collection Tanks

13- Oil Collection System with Transfer System

Dewatering Process Advantages

 60% to 85% Recovery of the aqueous phases from the waste mud

 40% reduction of waste volume and transportation.

 The water phase contained into the waste volume is almost totally recovered and resent back to the rig for new mud mixing, rig activities etc. This will drastically reduce

the cost of transportation for the water needed for operations.

 Reduction in waste pit size and smallest foot print

 Produced water should be tested for different ions.

Cutting Transfer System

Cuttings transfer system is required to collect all the solids discharge from solids control

equipment. This system is installed under Shale Shaker and Centrifuge Area.

Cutting Treatment Process

Cuttings treatment is important part of the waste management services which is usually done to

solidification and stabilize and fix the liquid phase movement on the solids.

Solidification refers to techniques that encapsulate the waste in a monolithic solid of high

structural integrity. In this treatment, material is added to reduce free water and possibly reduce

or slow potential contaminant release. Contaminant migration is restricted by vastly decreasing

the surface area exposed to leaching by isolating the wastes within an impervious capsule.

Stabilization refers to those techniques that reduce the hazard potential of wastes by converting

contaminants into their least soluble, mobile, or toxic form. The physical nature and handling

characteristics of the waste are not necessarily changed by stabilization.

General Quality, Health, Safety and Environmental

Requirements

1.1 COMPANY is committed to conducting operations in a manner which minimizes the risks to the

health and safety of those involved in, and affected by, such operations. COMPANY is also

committed to conducting its operations in a manner which minimizes the impact on natural and

‘built’ environments in the area of operations.

1.2 This section sets out minimum Quality, Health, Safety and Environmental (QHSE) requirements

for the WORK that CONTRACTOR and subcontractors will be required to comply with whilst

conducting operations for COMPANY.

1.3 COMPANY may perform audits and assessments of CONTRACTOR’s and its subcontractors’

performance under the CONTRACT. COMPANY reserves the right without prior notice to

conduct quality, health, safety, security and environmental audits of the Operations.

1.4 CONTRACTOR shall at all times be responsible for performing the WORK in a manner so as to

protect against loss of life, injury to people, damage to property and the environment. The

detailed manner means and methods of performing the WORK shall be under the control and

direction of CONTRACTOR.

1.5 Prior to the start of Operations, certain QHSE processes must be completed. These QHSE

processes include developing the final QHSE plan format and frequency of any QHSE audits,

permitting and licensing and COMPANY’s approvals. CONTRACTOR shall ensure that

sufficient resources are dedicated to this task to ensure completion prior to mobilization.

1.6 COMPANY may at any time stop the WORK or any portion of the WORK without penalty to

COMPANY if the WORK is not conducted in accordance with the Quality, Health, Safety or

Environmental guidelines specified in this CONTRACT or by the laws of the appropriate

jurisdiction. COMPANY reserves the right to require CONTRACTOR to remove any person from

the Operations in the case of non-compliance with QHSE procedures. COMPANY may also

prohibit the use of equipment and plant in cases where that equipment or plant does not meet

the required standards or is deemed unsafe.

1.7 CONTRACTOR shall ensure that activities associated with this CONTRACT are conducted in

compliance with applicable national and local HSE laws and regulations, CONTRACTOR’s

standards and COMPANY’s requirements and IADC documents.

1.8 Limits of Authority

COMPANY’s Representative(s) do not have the authority to relax health, safety or environment

standards. If they judge the Operation to be inadequate with regards to any aspect of quality,

health, safety, security or the environment, then the operation may be immediately suspended.

1.9 QHSE Plan

CONTRACTOR shall be required to develop a project-specific QHSE plan to manage the

WORK and associated risks. This plan shall, as a minimum, address topics included in the list

detailed in Item 7. Where existing CONTRACTOR QHSE publications detail the topics listed, it

is sufficient to either include them verbatim, or provide reference to where they may be found in

CONTRACTOR’s QHSE Manuals. The final project-specific QHSE plan shall satisfy

CONTRACTOR standards, COMPANY requirements and applicable HSE laws and regulations.

1.10 Contractor should establish an effective HSE system and implement it strictly.

1.11 Contractor must adhere to the standards and regulations of Health, Safe and Environment

constituted by the local government.

1.12 Contractor must strictly adhere to the procedures / standards and regulations of Health, Safety

and Environment promulgated by NIOC.

1.13 Contractor should comply with all applicable local government regulations including guidelines

and recommended practices and any other Iranian authorities having jurisdiction.

1.14 All personnel should respect the local culture, traditions and social values of the indigenous

population of the Iran and South Azadegan region.

1.15 Contractor shall hire qualified and experienced worker and familiar his personnel with hazards

especially with H2S and Mine and UXO (unexploded ordinance) according to related procedure.

1.16 Contractor shall conduct proper training about UXO (unexploded ordinance) and mine awareness

to all personnel

Health &amp;amp; Safety Management

2.1 CONTRACTOR shall take all necessary safety measures in relation to the WORK to be provided

and shall conduct itself and its work force in such a way as to comply at all times with the

provisions of the national and local health and safety regulations pertinent to the contracted

Scope of WORK.

CONTRACTOR shall also comply with all and any of COMPANY’S safety standards and

requirements as particularly applicable to the Operating Area and/or as may be imposed by

COMPANY from time to time.

2.2 CONTRACTOR and its subcontractors shall take all necessary steps to provide a safe and

healthy working environment for employees, COMPANY’s personnel and third parties in the

performance of the WORK.

2.3 CONTRACTOR shall operate a management system that ensures:

 CONTRACTOR’s and sub contractors’ personnel operate safe systems of work at all times

for the performance of the WORK.

 Hazards associated with the work are identified and appropriate actions are taken to prevent

accidents.

 Risks to safety and health of personnel involved in the WORK are identified and assessed,

and any actions and measurements are implemented to either eliminate or reduce these

risks to a level that is considered as low as reasonably practicable, given the nature of the

WORK.

 Personnel are aware of the identified hazards and risks to their safety and health and

to the safety and health of others.

The detailed safe working procedures established by CONTRACTOR to ensure that the WORK

will be conducted with due regard to the QHSE aspects, shall be detailed or referenced in the

QHSE plan. Copies of the source documents shall be provided to COMPANY if the procedures

are referenced only.

2.4 QHSE Representative

CONTRACTOR shall provide a dedicated, on-crew QHSE Representative. The primary

responsibility of the QHSE Representative shall include, but not be limited to, the maintenance

and monitoring of the implementation of HSE guidelines and procedures and where required

provide HSE awareness training for the crew. Such activities shall be approved and actively

supported by CONTRACTOR at corporate management level.

2.5 Training and Competence

CONTRACTOR shall ensure that its employees and those of its subcontractors are trained and

competent to perform the WORK in a safe and environmentally responsible manner. Such

Well: AZNS??? (S358)

Type : ISHPS

Health, Safety and Environment Page 90 of 117

Development Well

training and demonstration of competence will include but not be limited to vocational, safety,

emergency, environmental and other training required by regulatory agencies and IADC/OGP

guidelines. CONTRACTOR’s senior staff shall have received training in safety management.

2.6 Accident/Incident Reporting

2.6.1 CONTRACTOR shall report all incidents. Any hazardous incident / near miss involving

COMPANY, CONTRACTOR’s, or any third party personnel, plant or equipment, shall be

reported to COMPANY, whether or not injury to personnel or damage/loss to plant or equipment

resulted.

The following incidents shall be verbally reported immediately to the on-site COMPANY

Representative(s): damage to property or equipment belonging to COMPANY, third parties,

CONTRACTOR or its subcontractors; injury to personnel of COMPANY, third parties,

subcontractors or CONTRACTOR; near misses which may have had serious potential

consequences; environmental damage; media attention. An initial written report, containing

factual information only, shall be prepared by CONTRACTOR and delivered to the COMPANY

Representative within twenty-four (24) hours of the incident.

COMPANY reserves the right to participate in the investigation of any incident arising out of

performance of the WORK.

2.6.2 CONTRACTOR and all subcontractors shall have an accident and incident reporting system,

details of which shall be included in the QHSE plan.

2.6.3 CONTRACTOR shall maintain and forward to COMPANY, a monthly summary of its safety

performance.

2.6.4 CONTRACTOR shall prepare and forward to COMPANY, a weekly report stating all incidents

and near misses that occurred during that week.

2.6.5 All CONTRACTOR and subcontractor personnel and COMPANY Representative shall attend

safety meetings on a regular basis. COMPANY Representative shall be given a copy of the

minutes of the meetings.

2.7 Safety Equipment &amp;amp; Personal Protective Equipment (PPE) requirements

CONTRACTOR shall ensure that appropriate PPE compliant to national standards is supplied

to CONTRACTOR’s personnel involved in the WORK. COMPANY intends to use water-based

mud and hence CONTRACTOR’s Personnel that may be exposed to this must be provided with

appropriate PPE.

CONTRACTOR shall be solely liable for all costs in equipping its own and all other personnel

on contract to, or under the control of, CONTRACTOR with the required safety equipment.

CONTRACTOR will provide to all CONTRACTOR’s Personnel appropriate PPE throughout the

WORK. Any defective PPE will be replaced without delay. Personnel without the correct PPE

will not be allowed to continue work, until replacement PPE has been issued. If any person is

disallowed to work owing to inadequate PPE then COMPANY will deduct his day-rate from

CONTRACTOR’s invoice.

CONTRACTOR shall detail in the QHSE plan the PPE proposed for issue to the crew. This

proposal should include the arrangements for replacement (due to wear and tear and damage)

of this equipment.

2.8 Health

2.8.1 This well is committed to develop for oil in a responsible manner that promotes employees' and

local inhabitants' health.

2.8.2 A trained Paramedic and proper MEDIVAC equipment and ambulance shall be stationed on the

drilling rig during the drilling campaign.

2.8.3 An emergency medical evacuation plan shall be coordinated, and shall keep in touch with

emergency response centres manned by qualified doctor 24-hours per day in Tehran or Ahwaz

city.

2.8.4 Medical emergency report and investigation shall be performed as soon as possible in

accordance with Medical Emergency Procedures.

2.8.5 CONTRACTOR shall, as a minimum, conduct the WORK and provide personnel, equipment,

facilities and services consistent with good oil field practices and OGP Guidelines. An

assessment of the Contract Area with regard to “Environmental and Occupational Health Risks”

and “Local Medical Support” shall be provided by CONTRACTOR in the QHSE plan. This

assessment and the resulting “Health Risk Assessment Score” will be discussed with

COMPANY, and a final assessment will be agreed.

2.8.6 To ensure the fitness of those personnel engaged to undertake the WORK, CONTRACTOR

shall conduct periodic medical examinations of its staff and ensure that its subcontractors’

personnel are also subject to periodic medical examination. As a minimum this shall include

pre-hire medical examinations of temporary local personnel and bi-annual medical

examinations of its permanent and expatriate staff. Evidence should be made available to

COMPANY to verify an appropriate program of immunizations consistent with the

recommendations of the World Health Organization for the area of operations.

2.8.7 CONTRACTOR shall be responsible for the cost of medical examinations as required to provide

COMPANY with medical certificates which confirm the fitness of CONTRACTOR’s assigned

personnel for the WORK. Each drilling rig shall be equipped with a hospital, doctor and an

ambulance type-2 level.

2.8 Drug Abuse and Control

2.9.1 CONTRACTOR warrants that it shall obtain valid written consent of all Personnel to a search of

property of such Personnel when COMPANY suspects that such Personnel have in their

possession drugs and/or other prohibited substances or any other item likely to prejudice safety

or security. COMPANY will provide a representative to attend the search.

2.9.2 CONTRACTOR warrants that it shall obtain valid written consent of all Personnel to medical

testing for the presence of drugs or other prohibited substances.

2.9.3 CONTRACTOR further agrees that any Personnel who refuse to provide such consent or

consents or withdraws such consent or consents shall at COMPANY’s option be removed from

the WORK and shall not, at COMPANY’s option, be assigned to any future services to be

provided by CONTRACTOR for COMPANY.

2.9.4 CONTRACTOR further agrees that it shall immediately notify COMPANY in the event that

CONTRACTOR becomes aware that any of its Personnel has been found in possession of

drugs and/or any other prohibited substance. CONTRACTOR is required to notify COMPANY

in the event that any Personnel provided for the WORK have previously been found in

possession, or have abused drugs and/or other substances prohibited at Location. The

acceptance of such Personnel for the WORK shall be at the sole discretion of COMPANY.

2.9 Camp Standards

CONTRACTOR shall abide by all statutory Iranian governmental health and safety regulations,

and maintain acceptable standards for cleanliness and hygiene for quarters, camps and the

Operating Area(s) in general.

CONTRACTOR shall detail the standards and facilities proposed for its camp in the QHSE plan.

2.10 Emergency Preparedness

CONTRACTOR and its subcontractors shall have emergency preparedness and response

plans plus procedures available at all times throughout the duration of the CONTRACT.

CONTRACTOR shall take all necessary measures to ensure that:

I. Potential emergency situations have been identified and assessed;

II. An emergency response plan, including clear responsibilities, is established;

III. CONTRACTOR’s management, employees and third parties are aware of response

procedures and responsibilities;

IV. Regular drills and exercises are carried out to test the emergency response

organization and procedures and that any resultant issues are properly addressed.

CONTRACTOR shall notify COMPANY immediately with respect to any emergency situation and

shall provide regular updates on emergency planning procedures, tests etc.

CONTRACTOR shall detail in the QHSE plan a list of potential contingencies for which

emergency response procedures are planned. The QHSE plan should also detail a schedule of

proposed exercises and drills to verify the effectiveness of the proposed arrangements in place.

Development and implementation of the above plan shall incorporate any COMPANY assigned

personnel to the project.

Environmental Management

3.1 CONTRACTOR shall comply with any and all laws, rules and regulations of governmental

agencies having jurisdiction, which now exist or may be promulgated during the term of the

CONTRACT, relating to the control and prevention of damage to the environment.

3.2 Without prejudice to Item 3.1 above, CONTRACTOR shall ensure that all personnel use the

highest prevailing standards of care and diligence to prevent unlawful disposal of all waste oil,

water and other waste materials or fluids which may accumulate or be caused by

CONTRACTOR’s performance of the CONTRACT. CONTRACTOR shall not dispose of any

objects, scrap, waste, rubbish, equipment, fluids or fuel into the surrounding area or allow

anything to be stored loose where it can be driven into surrounding areas by wind.

CONTRACTOR shall take all reasonable precautions to prevent pollution of any nature or kind

from its performance of the WORK. CONTRACTOR shall have contingency plans and

procedures in place for such events.

3.3 In the event of any disposal prohibited by Item 3.2 above, whether by dumping, spilling, emission

or otherwise arising from CONTRACTOR’s performance or non-performance of the

CONTRACT, CONTRACTOR shall be fully liable for and bear the costs of removal of any such

item(s) dumped, spilled, emitted or otherwise disposed regardless of the sole, concurrent or

contributory negligence or breach of duty (statutory or otherwise) of COMPANY. In the event

of any such disposal, CONTRACTOR shall immediately undertake all reasonable remedial and

protective measures required in such circumstances.

ecology and disturbance of wildlife and farm animals;

 recycling and waste disposal;

occupied during the survey.

rubbish, food scraps, office waste and other discarded material from camps, created by the

disposed of, according to COMPANY approved methods or existing methods established by

CONTRACTOR, which are proven to be acceptable to COMPANY. Well location and camp site

shall be delivered to COMPANY without any waste, rubbish, food scraps, office waste and other

discarded material. COMPANY has the right to hold Completion certificate till cleaning the well

location and camp site by CONTRACTOR.

1) The access road and well location construction shall be accorded with construction

program of well location. It minimizes any impacts on the local residents, prevent all soil

contamination, prevent all ground water contamination, and prevent all surface water

contamination. Cutting and waste water pit shall be lining and drainage system shall be

good and to avoid farm and season canal contamination.

1) In drilling operation ，keep mud and sewage from overflowing. If it happens, clean up

3) Make full use of waste water. Water in mud pump and water brake must be utilized by

prevent kicks and blowout. Gas shall be guided into ignition equipment to burn it away.

allowed. For cement or barite that may result in granular dust pollution，operation shall be

performed in a closed system.

10) For abandoned well，filled with cement plugs to seal the oil, gas or water formation.

Well Control

(1) Recording Reference

All BOP tests, drills, function tests, any malfunctions, repair or maintenance to the mud system and

well control equipment shall be recorded in the IADC daily reports and shall be signed by both the

Drilling Contractor's Tool Pusher and Drilling Supervisor on the well site. They shall also be recorded

in the Daily Drilling Report.

(2) Flow Check Procedure

To avoid a kick situation, it is recommended that a flow check be conducted at specific times during

the drilling operation.

1) While drilling(any warning sign)

2) Prior to tripping the drill string

3) While tripping(any warning sign)

4) While out of hole(continuously)

5) Trip out:

 After pulling 5% of the drill pipe

 At the midpoint depth of the wellbore

 Prior to pulling the first stand of drill collars

 After all the drill string is out of hole.

6) Trip in:

 Upon reaching surface casing shoe

 At midpoint of the wellbore

(3) Primary Control Reference

It is the responsibility of the Drilling Contractor to keep the hole full at all times by using all the

available equipment. The Drilling Supervisor shall monitor that the correct preventive practices are

being implemented and/or conducted at all times.

Extreme care shall be taken to monitor mud volume, drilling breaks and gas cut mud.

The MACP (Maximum allowable casing pressure) shall be confirmed by the Drilling Supervisor, either

after setting each new casing string or, whenever the density of the drilling mud changes.

The MACP shall be clearly written on a kick control sheet that will be posted near the choke control

panel.

Well: AZNS??? (S358)

Type : ISHPS

Health, Safety and Environment Page 101 of 117

Development Well

The predetermined distance at which the Tool Joint is to be pulled above the rotary should be posted

on rig floor near the BOP control panel.

The Driller is responsible for carefully measuring and recording the slow pump pressure. Awareness of

these values is an important element in killing operations, in order to avoid formation breakdown.

Take a measure and record the slow pump pressure at three different flow rates with all pumps at the

following times:

 Once per tour.

 Whenever changes occur in the mud density/rheology.

 Whenever changes occur in the dimension and characteristics of the string , BHA, jet

size, jet plugged or jet lost, etc.

(4) Secondary Control Reference

Any time a drilling break is noticed, drilling shall stop and a static monitoring of the well shall be carried

out. Space-out for all Pipe Rams and Annular Preventer must be known to the Driller at all times to

keep tool joints clear of Annular preventer and rams.

(5) Kick Detection Procedure While Drilling

If there is an indication of a possible kick occurring, the Driller shall follow the kick detection

procedures outlined below:

Stop drilling with the pump onPick up top drive to a predetermined space-out positionShut off the

pumps and check for well flow by visual examination on the bell nipple or flow line.

1) If the well is not flowing:

 Immediately notify the rig manager, Drilling Supervisor and Toolpusher.

 The Drilling Supervisor will decide whether to circulate bottoms up or resume drilling. The

penetration rate will be limited as long as the cause of the abnormal situation is

undetermined. Always be aware that some hydrocarbons (gas or oil cut mud) may rise to

the surface. Ensure that the proper monitoring and safety systems all in place.

2) If the well is flowing:

 Close the well as per the Shut-in Procedure.

 Immediately notify the Wellsite Drilling Supervisor, rig manager and Toolpusher.

H2S Monitoring and Prevention Measure

9.1 Monitoring H2S

1) Somewhere on the well site is apt to accumulate H2S, such as mud pit, shale shaker, and

drilling platform, should be installed one set of. Make sure H2S monitor and alarm system

can be used.

2) Alarm threshold setting for H2S warning indicator: the first warning level is 10mg/m3, unable

warning sound, just show present value of H2S content to operators. The second warning

level is 20mg/m3, when the H2S content in air exceed 20mg/m3, H2S warning indicator can

give an alarm automatically, and warning sound should be heard by everyone work in well

site. The audible alarm should be installed on Kelly board.

3) Equip enough gas masks on drilling platform, shaker screen (total amount should large than

24), and equip 12 sets of 30min positive pressure Air Aspirators，2 sets of portable H2S

monitor and appropriative H2S warning indicator. Arrange a person specially assigned for

taking charge all the protective apparatus. This man must do periodical check to keep these

equipment in a good condition.

Well: AZNS??? (S358)

Type : ISHPS

Health, Safety and Environment Page 102 of 117

Development Well

4) H2S monitor should be done weekly check and compulsory examination.

5) After drilling into the pay zone, strengthen the detection of H2S content in mud.

6) Contractor shall implement H2S service system by himself or 3rd party for controlling the risk

of H2S leakage.

9.2 Measures for H2S prevention

1) Windsocks shall be set on the drill floor and in the monsoon direction or/and prevailing wind

direction of Azadegan oil field, off well site. In emergency, employees must follow the

evacuation procedure.

2) Explosion-proof blowers shall be installed around the rig, shale shaker, and drilling fluid tanks

to blow away toxic and flammable gas at the well site.

3) The rig crew in the H2S area shall be furnished with H2S monitors and adequate protective

apparatus in accordance with IPS. Everybody shall be able to use, maintain, and check the

apparatus.

4) The technicians of rig crew are responsible for H2S proof training and the tool pusher is in

charge of the supervision and examination.

5) The pH value of drilling fluid in the H2S area shall be kept over 9.5. Pay attention to

measurement of H2S concentration in drilling fluid. The H2S concentration shall be kept at

less than 50mg/cm3 by using sulphur removal agents and degassers.

6) Personnel safety and protection shall be carried out according to IPS during operations in

polluted areas where H2S content exceeds the critical concentration.

7) When the rig crew makes decision to bleed and fire because well control cannot be performed

due to well site conditions, operations shall be performed according to procedures in IPS.

8) Once blowout occurs, the incident shall be reported to the higher level department as soon as

possible and fire fighting trucks, ambulances, medical care personnel and technicians shall be

on duty at the well site.

9) After the blowout is controlled, H2S concentration should be measured in the places where

H2S is possible to gather.

10) The personnel are not allowed to access until the H2S concentration is below the critical

concentration.

مقدمه

3آخرین ویرایش: شهریور 1400

.1مقدمه

.1این نوشته به کوشش ناظرین عملیات منطقه یک معاونت فنی (حفاری) و با نظارت و راهنمایی جناب آقای مصاحب، مشاور معاونت فنی

(حفاری)، انجام شده است.

.2موارد به ترتیب اولویت اهمیت با رنگهای زیر دسته بنده شدهاند:

oقرمز (بیشترین اهمیت: هشداری)

oسیاه (اهمیت بالا: بالاترین فراوانی در متن)

oآبی (مهم: اهمیت زمانی کم)

oسبز (کم اهمیت: توضیحی، آموزشی و یا توصیهای)

.3منظور از علامت ↻ یعنی هماهنگیهایی که حضوری یا تلفنی باید با دیگران انجام شود.

.1کارگران محلی

4 .2-1نفر رادیومن و 8نفر محلی (شامل افراد ثابت و محلیهای ساکن محل چاه)

.3-1مطابق توافقنامه، ملی حفاری هزینه تعداد 8نفر کارگر را تقبل میکند، تایم شیفت مابقی ماهیانه توسط کمپانیمن امضا میشود.

.2درخواست آب آشامیدنی قبل از اثبات دکل مشکلی ندارد.

.3برای بلند کردن کالر ¾" 9از کلی استفاده میشود، و AIR HOISTنمیتواند آنرا بلند کند.

.4بهتر است منیفولدهای استند پایپ بصورتی باشد که اگر یک ولو خراب شد، قابلیت تعویض از دو سر OUTLETرا داشته باشند.

.5در اولین و آخرین تیکت مادلاگینگ بایستی این جمله نوشته شود: در بازهی زمانی R/Dو R/UPمادلاگینگ، هیچ هزینهای پرداخت نمیشود؛ بنابر این برای حضور زودتر مادلاگینگ با اداره هماهنگ شود.

.6بعضی از دکلها جهت گردش معکوس از لوله روی شیکر به سمت چوک یک پلاگ نصب کردهاند که بسیار ناایمن است و باید توسط یک زنجیر به بدنه لوله متصل شود.

.7در دکلهای خصوصی قید شود: تأیید این فرم توسط ناظر عالی معاونت فنی (حفاری) صرفاً به منظور ارائه گزارش راجع به وضعیت دکل است و تأیید نهایی اثبات دکل پس از بررسی مسائل قراردادی توسط مجری محترم طرح اعلام میگردد.

.20طراحی جداریها به شکلی انجام شود، که کاپلینگ آخرین اتصال حداقل یک متر زیر فلنج مبنا قرار گیرد.

.21برای فلوت کردن از پمپ تراک استفاده میکنیم، چون ممکن است به فشار زیادی احتیاج داشته باشد.

.23برنامه سیمان

.1-23هد دالیز درزمانیکه سیمان به طور کامل جابجا میشود، باید از هد داخل جداری بیشتر باشد، و الا به سمت بیرون یوتیوب میکند.

جداری ”8/3 13

19آخرین ویرایش: شهریور 1400

.2-23معمولاً در برنامه سیمان متراژ سیمان دنباله 152متر در دالیز درنظر گرفته میشود. (با درنظر گرفتن 2شاخه شوترک و اضافه درصد حجمی حفره باز) همچنین بعد از اتمام جابجایی سیمان فشار ستون دالیز حداقل باید 100پام بیشتر از داخل جداری باشد. (جلوگیری از Uکردن به دالیز)

.3-23مقدار سیمان اضافه 20% CSG-CSGو برای 100% OPEN HOLE-CSGدرنظر گرفته میشود. شمردن استروکها با سنگ حین جابجایی سیمان (دکلبان روی پمپ)

.24چک کردن هد سیمانکاری (رزوهها، باز و بست خروسک، و تعداد دور بسته شدن STEMمعمولاً 12دور)

.25سیلوها 750و 1050فوت مکعب است، برای تبدیل آن به کیسه به 1.19تقسیم میکنیم: 880و630کیسه

.26چک کردن استروک کانترهای ملی حفاری و مادلاگینگ

.27سیمان راهنما معمولاً بنتونایتی و سیمان دنباله خالص است.

.28بهتر است که تعداد لودهای طرف سیلوی دنباله کمتر باشد، در غیر اینصورت پمپ تراک طرف دنباله زودتر شروع کند.

.29اگر BOPداشتیم برای بلند کردن آن جهت نصب سیلیپس هماهنگ شود. اما از آنجا که اندازه سیلیپس بزرگ است و انداختن آن از روی فلور باعث شکستن آن میشود، تقریبا همیشه BOPرا هنگ میکنیم.

.30اگر حین جابجایی سیمان برگشتی قطع شود، برای بلند کردن هایدریل احتیاج به مجوز رئیس منطقه و ملی حفاری است.

.31اگر سیمان برگشتی مشاهده نشود، یکی از پمپ تراکها را برای تاپ جاب نگه میداریم، و همینطور 4شیر پایین (مربوط به )BASE FLNGرا باز نمیکنیم.

GIN POLE .32برای کشیدن جداری زمان انداختن سیلیپس در محل باشد.

.33بعد از جاب درصورت زیاد آمدن آب در مخزن سلوشن آنرا توسط پمپ تراک به مخازن آب منتقل میکنیم.

.34برای TOP JOBاحتمالی، درصورت دریافت سیمان خالص، سلوشن آماده شده با بنتونایت در مخازن گل را به مخزن سلوشن انتقال میدهیم.

.35چک کردن CSG HD SPOOLتوسط PLUG TESTو وارونه کردن و اندازهگیری قطر داخلی آن و چک کردن SEAL

.36چک کردن اندازه ( W.BUSHبا درنظر گرفتن فاصله آن با TIE D.SCREWملی حفاری)

.37سفت کردن دو ولو در دو طرف CHSو آزمایش کردن آن در سطح توسط ولهد

.38جهت سلک کردن کل یا مقداری از وزن جداریها روی سیلیپس، با توجه به تحمل سیلیپس انجام میشود. (از ولهد سوال شود، معمولاً بیش از 300هزار پوند تحمل میکنند) اگر تحمل سیلیپس کمتر از وزن رشته باشد، با اعمال مقداری از وزن جداریها مابقی وزن روی سیمان قرار داده میشود.

.39برابر شدن وزن و ویس رفت و برگشت زمان گردش(قبل از جدا کردن سوییچ و سوار کردن هد سیمانکاری)، در غیر اینصورت از داخل لوله گل میریزد و باز و بستن ابزار مشکل است.

.40درصورت وجود BOPقبل از بلند کردن BOPبررسی باید از اسپول باشد، و نه از دهانه چاه، زیرا درصورت جریان داشتن سیمان وارد اسپول و BOPمیشود.

.41در هنگام چک کردن فلوت درصورت اوکی بودن شیرهای هد سیمانکاری باید باز بماند، که اگرجریان پیدا کرد متوجه شویم، و فشار نگیرد؛ زیرا بعداً ترخیص فشار باعث ایجاد یک فیلم نازک بین جداری و سیمان خواهد شد.

.42جهت اطمینان از گیر نکردن پلاگ درون هد سیمانکاری، IDآن چک شود.

.43

.44سیلیپس دستی و اتوماتیک

.1-44در صورت امکان همیشه از سیلیپس اتوماتیک استفاده شود؛ زیرا سیلیپس دستی هیچگونه عمل نگهدارندهای انجام نمیدهد.

.2-44سیلیپس دستی تنها جهت مرکز قرار دادن جداری و نشتبندی است.

.1زمان استفاده از سیلیپس دستی:

.2کافی نبودن وزن رشته جداری به دلیل بندش سریع سیمان

.3فلو کردن چاه و بستن رمز حین سیمانکاری

.3-44اگر امکان کشیدن حدود 60-80KLBوجود داشته باشد، ترجیحاً از سیلیپس اتوماتیک استفاده شود.

.1اگر کاپلینگ نزدیک مقر سیلیپس باشد و امکان کشیدن جداری به اندازه کافی وجود نداشته باشد، بالاجبار از سیلیپس دستی باید، بعد از بریدن جداری، استفاده کرد.

.4-44سیلیپس دستی 13 3/8دو تکه و 9 5/8یک تکه است.

.45قرار دادن سیلیپس در محل قبل از سیمانکاری

.1-45مزایا: دیگر خطر زود سفت شدن سیمان و عدم موفقیت در قرار دادن سیلیپس در محل وجود ندارد؛

.2-45ایراد اول: احتمال فلو کردن و عدم موفقیت در قرار دادن مجدد فورانگیرها در محل خود

.3-45ایراد دوم: مقدار پس فشار روی سیمان و هرزروی بیش از حد.

.46اگر برگشتی سیمان نداشتیم جهت SLACK OFFکامل وزن جداری روی سیلیپس هماهنگ شود، زیرا در این حالت به علت وزن بیشتر جداری احتمال مچالگی جداری وجود دارد؛ توصیه میشود %75وزن جداری سلک آف شود و با چند ساعت انتظار اجازه دهیم مابقی وزن به روی سازند اعمال شود.

PUMP TRUCK(2NO) .1یکی به عنوان استندبای

.2سیمان و افزایه و برنامه سیمان

SOL.TANK .3درصورت زیاد بودن حجم سیمان

.4نیپل (هاف ترد) دو عدد برای دو طرف اسپول (تهیه با ملی حفاری)

نکات و هماهنگی ها↻

.1مقدار حداکثر مجاز فشار برای پمپاژ سوال شود (توجه به فشار COLLAPSEجداری داخل و BURSTجداری بیرون)

.2انجام جاب در زمانیکه رشته حفاری داخل چاه است:

.1-2درصورتیکه وزن گل داخل جداری بیشتر از دالیز باشد، با هماهنگی اداره میتوان جاب را بدون بالا آمدن انجام داد.

.2-2اما باید تا دو=سه استند بالاتر از بالا بیاییم

.3-2در تمام مدت سیمانکاری حفار باید رشته را بالا پایین کند.

.4-2قبل از جاب کلی بک کرده و با سوییچ گردش شود

.5-2درصورت مشاهده درگ پمپاژ سیمان قطع شود، و سریعاً لولهها را تا سطح بالا میآوریم.

.3برای اطمینان از فشار نگرفتن دالیز میتوان یک المک (با چکسن که انتهای آن آزاد باشد، با Tو یک ولو به چکسن اصلی متصل شود و یا به خروجی یکی از ولوهای اسپول) وصل کرد.

.4ابتدا به صورت PRIMسیمان میزنیم و بعد از پر شدن چاه، ادامه سیمان کاری با فشاری کمتر از فشار حدمجاز انجام شود.

PRIM .5کردن سیمان به معنای باز بودن طرف دیگر اسپول (برای اطمینان از پر شدن چاه) است.

.6برای تزریقپذیری هماهنگ شود. اگر برگشتی حین پمپاژ سیمان داخل جداری به طور کامل قطع شده باشد و اگر وزن گل پشت جداری کم باشد، تزریقپذیری انجام نمیشود. (به جهت پر نکردن فضای دالیز)

.7اگر پمپاژ همراه با فشار باشد و سیمان از المک به بیرون پرتاب شود، میتوان کمی ولو المک را بست.

.8اگر برای گل سنگین انجام تاپ جاب به تاخیر افتاد جهت جلوگیری از چند مرتبه باید تزریقپذیری گرفته شود.

.9اگر وزن سیمان تاپ جاب کم باشد، تزریقپذیری نباید انجام شود؛ زیرا چاه پر از آب شده و سیمان کند پایین میرود.

.10اگر بعد از پمپاژ سیمان، دالیز پر نشود (یا از SIDE VALVEها مکش داشت)، جهت تکرار جاب هماهنگ شود.

.11اگر بدون فشار سیمان پمپ میشود، ریت را کمتر کرده و میتوان پمپاژ را همراه با چند مرتبه توقف انجام داد.

مراحل

.1تزریقپذیری در آخر 3بشکه آب میزنیم، سپس یکی از شیرهای یک طرف را میبندیم و یک بشکه آب میزنیم، و آنگاه شیرها را باز کرده طرف مقابلش را باز میکنیم و یک بشکه دیگر آب میزنیم.

.2بعد از پمپاژ سیمان شیرها را میبندیم که سیمان پایین نرود، و تا 24ساعت بعد آنرا باز نمیکنیم، زیرا سیمان میشکند.

.3بعد از 24ساعت باز میکنیم و وضعیت آنرا بررسی کرده و داخل شیرها را هم چک میکنیم.

.23برنامه سیمان

.1-23جهت جلوگیری از Uتیوب سیمان و خالی شدن آن، مجموع وزن سیمان باید مقدار کمی (حدود 100پیسیاف) از وزن گل بیشتر باشد.

.2-23سیمان جداری دارای دنباله و راهنما است.

.3-23دمای ته چاه در برنامه سیمان با دمای تحویل داده شده از لاگ مقایسه شود.

.4-23توصیه میشود، در زمان دریافت برنامه سیمان یک پایلوت تست درون آزمایشگاه گلشناس انجام شود. و گلشناس نمونه را درون OVENیا روی بخاری گرم کند. (براساس مقدار سفت شدن این نمونه میتوان یک ایدهی خوبی برای زمان بندش و نگرانی نبستن سیمان درون لولههای حفاری بدهد).

.24فشار هوای کمپروسور (پمپتراک) از 15تا 25باشد. هرچه وزن سیمان بالاتر باشد نیاز به فشار هوای بیشتری است.

.28دو شاخه اول را 53.5پوندی میبندیم:

.1-28برای آزمایش ولهد باید از جداری مطمئن استفاده کرد، همین طور است برای وزن جداری که روی سیلیپس انداخته میشود. باید از جداری با بالاتری مقاومت استفاده شود.

.2-28دلیل دوم سوراخ شدگی جداری که این اتفاق اگر در سطح باشد نسبت به عمق پایین بسیار خطرناکتر است.

.29گردش گل درون شو جهت SHEARکردن گل و از بین بردن GELگل است.

.30در گل سنگین با YPبالا جهت گردش ابتدا به مدت 10دقیقه گردش آرام کرده که YIELDگل شکسته شود.

.31اندازهگیری قطر بیرونی LANDING JOINTو امتحان کردن سیلیپس روی آن

.32مقدار انیدریت حفاری شده و مقدار باقیمانده (که حفاری نشده است) از زمین شناس پرسیده شود. (جهت مشخص کردن فاصله کفشک از ته چاه)

.33در صورت ترکیدن هد سیمانکاری سریعاً توسط آچار دکل هد را با کاپلینگ متصل به آن باز کرده و کنار میگذاریم (نیازی به باز کردن یک شاخه و بالا آمدن نیست) باید از قبل یک عدد کاپلینگ مناسب اضافه آماده باشد.

.34قانوناً در سیمآنهای هایدنس گروه تخلیه باید حضور داشته باشد، تیکت کمپرسور پمپتراک را میتوان شارژ نکرد.

.35در صورت ژلاپ شدن سیمان (افزایش فشار) در هنگام پمپاژ ابتدا وزن را کم میکنیم. و سرعت پمپاژ را پائین میآوریم. سپس وزن را به مقدار واقعی میرسانیم.

.36درصورت بالا بودن وزن سیمان پایلوت آزمایش در آزمایشگاه گلشناسی انجام شود.

.37درصورت بالا بودن وزن سیمان تأکید شود که پمپتراک با کیفیت بهتر ارسال شود.

.38معمولاً در برنامه سیمان متراژ سیمان دنباله 152متر در دالیز درنظر گرفته میشود. (با درنظر گرفتن 2شاخه شوترک و اضافه درصد حجمی حفره باز) همچنین بعد از اتمام جابجایی سیمان فشار ستون دالیز حداقل باید 100پام بیشتر از داخل جداری باشد. (جلوگیری از Uکردن به دالیز)

.4از زمینشناس عمق و طول GAS CAPسوال شود.

:BULL HD .7با بالا آمدن گاز و فشار گرفتن، به اندازه 1.3-1.5برابر حجم چاه بولهد میکنیم.

.8هرزروی کامل:

.1-8اگر احتمال هرزروی بالا یا کامل وجود داشته باشد، قبل از باز کردن شو یا (باهماهنگی) در فرصتی مناسب 10استند لوله ½" 3یا 2 "( 7/8بسته به سایز حفره) برای زدن سیمان (جهت کنترل هرزروی) آماده شود. درخواست رمز مناسب هم از قبل به رئیس دستگاه داده شود.

.2-8در صورت نیاز به آبگیری برای بلند کردن تعداد لولههای لازم باید توجه کرد که وزن لولهها بیشتر از نیروی روبه بالای ایجاد شده توسط فشار پمپاژ برای کشتن چاه باشد.

.3-8در هرزروی زیاد حین تریپ اگر نیاز به بررسی باشد، باید از تریپ تانک به صورت CROSS WELL HDگردش کرده و بعد از مدت زمان مشخص میزان هرزروی را از تریپ تانک حساب کرد.

.4-8اگر دکل پسماند نداشت، در موارد هرزروی کامل هماهنگی جهت پمپ مهندسی و ساختمان برای پمپاژ آب از گودال هرزآب به مخازن گل

.5-8درمواردی که احتمال هرزروی کامل وجود دارد، برای قرار دادن وربوشینگ در محل هماهنگ شود.

.6-8هنگام هرزروی کامل در مخزن نباید (جهت به هم نیامدن چاه) بالا بیاییم. مگر با هماهنگی؛ حتی بالا آمدن به اندازه دو، سه استند هم از جهت قطع کردن کانکشن و اتصال مجدد آن (یا کنار گذاشتن/برداشتن کلی) زمان بر است و موجب از دست دادن گل زیاد میشود.

.7-8همیشه به اندازه حداقل 100بشکه HI.VIS PILLدر ذخیره آماده باشدو تعداد کافی کیسههای SHELL FISHروی مخازن آماده باشد.

.8-8در هنگام هرزروی کامل تا آماده شدن ،LCM PILLبالا پایین قطع نشود (احتمال گیر)

.9-8هماهنگی جهت استفاده از مته سنترجت

.10-8برای انجام BY PASSگل قبل از رسیدن به عمق پیشبینی هرزروی درون برنامه چاه و یا چاههای اطراف توجه شود و یا هماهنگ شود.

.11-8درزمانیکه گل به صورت BY PASSپمپاژ میشود جهت تریپ لوله بالا برای جلوگیری از پلاگ شدن لولهها گل فرش باید داخل رشته پمپاژ شود.

.12-8در زمان مناسب بعد از اطلاع از هرزروی کامل خط آتش چک شود که به دلیل باز بودن شیر آن گزارش اشتباه هرزروی کامل داده شده باشد.

.13-8درصورت نیاز به بالا بردن خیلی زیاد ویسکازیته پیل LCMجهت پمپاژ از مسیر هپر استفاده شود، چرا که مسیر مستقیم مکش خوبی ندارد، پیل با سرعت کمی پمپ میشود و احتمال دارد پمپ هوا بزند.

.14-8در صورت احتمال استاک (تصمیم به پمپاژ حجم زیاد با پنداژ بالا) بهتر دو سه استند بالا آمده و سپس پیل اثبات شود.

.9حفاری مخزن به خصوص لایههای لایمستون از قبل به حفار گوشزد شود، بعد از هر تعمیرات مانند واش پایپ، دراورکس یا بعد از کانکشن، حین پایین رفتن وزن بیش از 5هزار خطر استاک وجود دارد.

.10بعد از حفاری برای بالا آمدن، جهت جلوگیری از ،SWABدو یا سه شاخه را با پمپاژ بالا میآییم.

.11درصورت پلاگ شدن رشته نباید در حفره باز اقدام به UNPLUGکرد، تنها چند مرتبه اعمال فشار کرده و در شو اقدام به UNPLUGشود

.12جهت زدن لایمستون چیپس که دارای دانهبندی درشت و سنگین است زمانیکه رشته از چاه بیرون آمده است، کیسههای را پاره کرده و نفرات، از روی فلور یا روی فلو لاین، آنها را داخل دهانه چاه خالی میکنند.

.13پمپاژ LCMبالاتر از 50پوند به غیر از احتمال پلاگ شدن رشته خطر پلاگ شدن مسیر از تانکیها گل تا استندپایپ و خطوط KILL LINE مربوط به BOPرا هم دارد.

.14اگر جهت هرزروی زیاد تصمیم به پمپاژ LCMبا پنداژ بالا گرفته شود به شرط باز بودن نازلهای مته PIPE SCREENقرار داده نشود.

.15به هنگام فلو چک اگر نرخ جریان زیاد باشد مدت بررسی باید کوتاه باشد و درون گزارش به واحد بشکه در ساعت آورده شود.

.16حین بالا آمدن از تریپ تانک پر شود، اما اگر به دلایلی مثل هرزروی زیاد یا وزن زیاد گل نمیتوان از تریپ تانک گردش داشت، حداقل 20 استروک داخل دالیز پمپاژ شود.

.17ضرورت بررسی روی کالر

.1-17در زمان بالا آمدن اگر کالرها از نوع اسپیرال SPIRALباشد، ضرورت بررسی روی کالر دوچندان است؛ زیرا هایدریل نمیتواند این نوع کالر را به خوبی نشتبندی کند.

.2-17به این دلیل که کالر TOOL JOINTندارد که در رمها گیر کند، در زمان فلو این امکان وجود دارد که لولهها به بیرون پرتاب شوند.

.3-17به دلیل سطح مقطع بزرگتر حین بیرون آوردن کالر حجم بیشتری از چاه خالی میشود.

.4-17به دلیل سطح مقطع بزرگتر حین بیرون آوردن کالر اگر جریان وجود داشته باشد نیروی رو به بالای بیشتری به رشته اعمال میکند.

.1آهک

.1-1پایدارکننده ELECTRICAL STABILITYاست. بین 200-500است. ESکم یعنی فاز آب به خوبی با فاز OILپوشانده نشده است. با حفاری و خرد شدن ذرات توسط شیرینگ این عدد بالاتر میرود.
